{".gitignore":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"node_modules/\nout/\nbuild/\n.DS_Store\n"},".prettierrc":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"{\n  \"useTabs\": false,\n  \"bracketSpacing\": true,\n  \"tabWidth\": 2\n}\n"},"README.md":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"# RIFT Video — a code-driven video engine\n\nTurn a sentence into a rendered MP4. In RIFT, in any project:\n\n```\n/video 30-second vertical launch video for Acme, a Postgres backup tool, for developers\n```\n\nThe agent sets this engine up if the project doesn't have it (`rift video init`), writes a\nstoryboard, turns it into a typed spec, validates it, looks at every scene, and renders\n`out/<id>.mp4`. The engine ships inside RIFT, so nothing needs to be downloaded except its npm\ndependencies. Everything below is what that command drives, and how to use or\nextend it by hand.\n\n## What it is\n\nA video here is **data**: a `VideoSpec` in `src/data/videoSpecs/` that says what each scene\nshows and for how long. Scene components decide how things look. That split is what makes\nnatural-language generation reliable. A model only has to produce a small, validated data\nstructure, never a pile of bespoke animation code, and every video inherits improvements to the\nvisual system.\n\n```\nbrief → storyboard → VideoSpec (typed, validated) → scene components → Remotion → MP4\n```\n\n**Why Remotion:** videos are React components rendered frame by frame from `useCurrentFrame()`.\nThe same frame always renders the same pixels, it runs locally, it needs no paid service, and a\nvideo lives in git like any other code.\n\n## Install\n\nNeeds Node 20+ (tested on 24). FFmpeg is optional; it's used for contact sheets and blank-frame\nchecks, and Remotion ships its own for rendering.\n\n```bash\ncd video\nnpm install\n```\n\nThe first render downloads Chrome Headless Shell (~90 MB) once.\n\n## Commands\n\n| Command | What it does |\n| --- | --- |\n| `npm run dev` | Remotion Studio: live preview, scrubbing, and the spec as an editable form |\n| `npm run validate [-- <id>] [--deep]` | Spec rules, assets on disk, fonts; `--deep` also bundles and checks registration |\n| `npm run frames -- <id>` | One PNG per scene plus `contact-sheet.png` in `out/frames/<id>/` |\n| `npm run render [-- <id>] [--output out/x.mp4]` | Validate → render H.264 → verify the file (default: `ProductLaunch`) |\n| `npm run new -- <id> [--template T] [--format F]` | Start a video from a template and register it |\n| `npm test` | Unit tests for timing, captions, validation and the scripts |\n| `npm run lint` | ESLint (Remotion's config) and TypeScript |\n| `npm run build` | Typecheck and bundle for deployment |\n\nExamples:\n\n```bash\nnpm run render -- rift-launch                                     # 30s, 9:16\nnpm run render -- transformer-explainer                           # 90s, 16:9\nnpm run render -- ProductLaunch --output out/product-launch.mp4   # a template\n```\n\n## Preview\n\n`npm run dev` opens Studio. The sidebar has three kinds of entry:\n\n- **Templates**: `ProductLaunch`, `ExplainerVideo`, `ShortVideo`, the starting points for `npm run new`.\n- **Videos**: every spec in `src/data/videoSpecs/index.ts`.\n- **`<id>-scenes`**: each scene of a video on its own timeline, for tuning one scene without\n  scrubbing the rest.\n\nFor agent-driven work, `npm run frames` is the preview. Stills are taken once each scene has\nsettled, so they show what a viewer actually reads.\n\n## Render\n\n`npm run render` refuses to start if validation has errors. It renders with fixed settings\n(H.264, CRF 18, yuv420p), then checks the file with ffprobe against the spec: resolution, frame\nrate, duration within one frame, codec, audio present if the spec has audio, and no blank\nstretches. Any mismatch exits non-zero. Output goes to `out/`, which is git-ignored.\n\n## Writing a spec\n\n```ts\nimport { defineVideo, FORMATS } from \"../../spec/schema\";\n\nexport const acmeLaunch = defineVideo({\n  id: \"acme-launch\",\n  title: \"Acme — launch\",\n  format: FORMATS.vertical,            // or FORMATS.horizontal / FORMATS.square, 30 fps\n  targetDurationInSeconds: 30,         // validation fails if the scenes don't add up\n  style: { theme: \"terminal\", pacing: \"fast\", accent: \"#7C9CFF\" },\n  captions: { enabled: true },         // captions from each scene's `narration`\n  scenes: [\n    { id: \"hook\", type: \"statement\", durationInSeconds: 4, lines: [\"Backups fail *silently.*\"] },\n    {\n      id: \"proof\", type: \"terminal\", durationInSeconds: 6,\n      transition: { type: \"fade\", durationInSeconds: 0.4 },\n      narration: \"Acme restores every backup, every night.\",\n      lines: [{ kind: \"input\", text: \"acme verify --all\" }, { kind: \"success\", text: \"412 restores passed\" }],\n    },\n    { id: \"cta\", type: \"cta\", durationInSeconds: 4, wordmark: \"ACME\", headline: \"Know your backups work.\", url: \"acme.dev\" },\n  ],\n});\n```\n\nRules the engine enforces:\n\n- **Time.** A transition belongs to the scene it leads into and overlaps the scene before it,\n  so total = sum of durations − transitions. `targetDurationInSeconds` errors say exactly how\n  much to cut or add.\n- **Emphasis.** Wrap words in `*asterisks*` to set them in the accent colour, in scenes and captions.\n- **Assets.** Paths are relative to `public/` (e.g. `images/logo.svg`). Missing files fail validation.\n- **Fit.** Headlines, terminal lines and code lines are measured against the frame; copy that\n  would clip is an error.\n- **Reading time.** A scene shorter than it takes to read its words (about 4 words/s) is a\n  warning, and so is narration too fast to caption.\n\n### Themes\n\n| Theme | Use for | Look |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `terminal` | dev tools, launches, demos | near-black, quiet green accent, mono details |\n| `paper` | teaching, explainers, diagrams | warm off-white, blue accent |\n| `midnight` | cinematic typography, announcements | near-black, warm amber accent |\n\n`style.accent` sets a brand accent. `style.palette` overrides any role (`background`, `surface`,\n`surfaceRaised`, `border`, `text`, `textMuted`, `accent`, `danger`, `warning`, `success`) with a\nhex colour; roles you leave out come from the theme.\n\n### Scene catalog\n\n| Type | Use for | Fields |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `title` | hook, section opener | `eyebrow?`, `headline`, `subheadline?` |\n| `statement` | typographic beats, one line at a time | `lines[]` (max 5) |\n| `quote` | testimonial | `quote`, `author`, `role?` |\n| `terminal` | CLI demo; inputs type out, results follow | `heading?`, `title`, `lines[{kind: input\\|output\\|success\\|error\\|muted, text}]` |\n| `code` | a snippet with highlighted lines | `heading?`, `filename?`, `code`, `highlight?[]` (1-based) |\n| `browser` | a product page or screenshot in a browser frame | `heading?`, `url`, `image?` or `page{title, lines[]}` |\n| `list` | checklist, status list | `heading`, `items[{label, detail?, status?}]` |\n| `cards` | features, concepts (up to 6) | `heading?`, `cards[{title, body?, glyph?}]` |\n| `flow` | a process, with an optional loop back | `heading?`, `steps[]`, `loop?{from, to, label?}` |\n| `metrics` | numbers that count up | `heading?`, `metrics[{value, label, prefix?, suffix?, decimals?}]` |\n| `chart` | bar chart from data | `heading?`, `data[{label, value}]`, `unit?`, `highlight?`, `source?` |\n| `chips` | tokens, tags, IDs | `heading?`, `chips[{label, sub?}]`, `note?` |\n| `vectors` | embeddings, feature vectors (−1..1) | `heading?`, `rows[{label, values[]}]`, `note?` |\n| `heatmap` | attention, similarity (0..1), up to 4 matrices | `heading?`, `matrices[{title?, rows[], cols[], values[][]}]`, `note?` |\n| `media` | an image or video clip from `public/` | `heading?`, `src`, `caption?`, `fit?` (`contain`/`cover`) |\n| `cta` | the close | `logo?` or `wordmark?`, `headline`, `url?`, `subline?` |\n\nEvery scene also takes `id`, `durationInSeconds`, `narration?` and `transition?` (`fade`,\n`slide`, `wipe`, `none`; `durationInSeconds`; `direction?`).\n\n## Giving it a brief\n\n`/video` accepts anything from one line to a full brief with structure and timings. Useful\nthings to include: audience, platform or aspect ratio, length, tone, the one thing a viewer\nshould remember, the CTA, and any assets (drop them in `public/` first). Anything you leave out\ngets a sensible default, and the storyboard Claude shows you first is where to correct course.\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\nvideo/\n├── src/\n│   ├── spec/            schema.ts (VideoSpec), timing.ts, validate.ts — pure, tested\n│   ├── data/videoSpecs/ one file per video + index.ts registry and template map\n│   ├── scenes/          one component per scene type + registry.tsx\n│   ├── components/      Text, Effects, Layout, UI, Charts, Media, Captions — props only, no content\n│   ├── compositions/    SpecVideo (renders any spec), AudioTracks\n│   ├── lib/             animation, captions, typography (fonts), audio provider interface, context\n│   ├── styles/          theme.ts (palettes, motion), scale.ts (type scale, safe areas, fit)\n│   └── Root.tsx         registers compositions from the registry; never needs editing\n├── scripts/             validate, frames, render, new (+ lib/ with tests)\n└── public/              fonts/ (bundled, OFL), images/, audio/, assets/\n```\n\n## Reusable pieces\n\nComponents take props, never content, and read theme and sizes from context:\n\n- **Text:** `Display`, `Headline`, `Subheadline`, `Body`, `Label`, `Code`, `Eyebrow`, `Cta`, `Metric`, `Emphasis`\n- **Motion:** `Reveal` / `FadeIn` / `SlideIn`, `ScaleIn`, `WordReveal`, `TypeReveal`. One entrance curve\n  (`motion.enter`) and a pacing-aware `stagger()`, with no springs or bounce by design.\n- **UI:** `Window`, `Terminal`, `BrowserWindow`, `CodeEditor`, `Card`, `StatusGlyph`, `Cursor`, `Callout`, `ProgressBar`, `Notification`, `HighlightBox`\n- **Media:** `AssetImage`, `ImageReveal`, `VideoClip`\n- **Charts:** `BarChart`, `FlowSteps`, `Heatmap`, `VectorStrip`, `Chip`\n- **Captions:** `CaptionTrack`, in `@remotion/captions` format\n- **Layout:** `SceneFrame` (background plus platform safe area), `Stack`\n\n## Extending\n\n**A new scene type:** add its schema to `src/spec/schema.ts` (and to the `Scene` union), draw it\nin `src/scenes/`, map it in `scenes/registry.tsx`, and teach `validate.ts` what has to fit and\nwhat counts as reading time. Exhaustive `never` guards make TypeScript fail in both places until\nyou do.\n\n**Voiceover (ElevenLabs, Kokoro, local TTS):** implement `VoiceoverProvider` in\n`src/lib/audio/provider.ts`. It writes a file to `public/audio/` and returns its path, which goes\nin `spec.audio.voiceover`. The renderer only ever plays files, so it never depends on a provider.\n\n**Transcription (Whisper, AssemblyAI):** produce `Caption[]` and set\n`captions: { enabled: true, source: \"items\", items }`. Word-level captions are paged\nautomatically, with the spoken word highlighted.\n\n**Screenshots (Claude in Chrome or other browser automation):** capture to `public/images/` and\nuse a `browser` scene's `image`. Capture is a step before rendering, never part of it.\n\n**FFmpeg post-processing** (compression, loudness, burn-in, format conversion): run on\n`out/*.mp4` after `npm run render`. Remotion already handles encoding and audio mixing, so reach\nfor FFmpeg only for what it adds.\n\n**Manim, avatars (HeyGen), publishing, MCP tools:** render or fetch into `public/` as assets, or\npublish from `out/`. The core renderer stays independent of all of them.\n\n## Known limitations\n\n- **Fit is estimated.** Validation estimates text width from character counts; `npm run frames`\n  is the real check for clipping and overlap, and `/video` makes Claude look at every frame.\n- **No line charts or dashboards yet.** Bar charts, heatmaps, vectors and metrics exist. Add a\n  scene type for anything else (see Extending).\n- **Per-scene timing is duration plus transition.** Entrance timing comes from the pacing, not\n  from per-element keyframes in the spec; fine-tune in Studio or in the scene component.\n- **Two font families.** Inter and JetBrains Mono ship in `public/fonts`. A brand font means\n  adding its files and a `loadFont` entry in `src/lib/typography/fonts.ts`.\n- **No voice, transcription or capture providers ship.** The interfaces are there; pick a provider\n  when a video needs one.\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n| Symptom | Fix |\n| --- | --- |\n| Text renders in a serif font | The font loader was tree-shaken. `package.json` `sideEffects` must list `src/lib/typography/fonts.ts`. |\n| `scenes add up to Xs, brief asks for Ys` | Adjust durations by the amount given; remember transitions overlap. |\n| `wraps to N lines` / `characters, but N fit` | Shorten the copy; don't shrink the type below the scale. |\n| `missing file public/...` | Add the asset, or fix the path (relative to `public/`, no leading slash). |\n| Render exits with `blank frames` | A scene drew nothing for that stretch; check it with `npm run frames`. |\n| `blank-frame check could not run ffmpeg` | Neither system ffmpeg nor Remotion's bundled one ran; reinstall with `npm install`. |\n| First render hangs at \"Downloading Chrome\" | One-time ~90 MB download; needs network once. |\n"},"eslint.config.mjs":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"import { config } from \"@remotion/eslint-config-flat\";\n\nexport default [\n  ...config,\n  {\n    // Specs and tests describe scene transitions as data; the rule mistakes the `transition`\n    // key for a CSS transition. },\n];\n"},"package-lock.json":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"{\n  \"name\": \"rift-video\",\n  \"version\": \"1.0.0\",\n  \"lockfileVersion\": 3,\n  \"requires\": true,\n  \"packages\": {\n    \"\": {\n Narration-timed\n * captions are an estimate; a transcription provider (Whisper, AssemblyAI) replaces them by\n * supplying `captions.items` with real timings, and nothing downstream changes.\n */\nimport type { Caption, VideoSpec } from \"../../spec/schema\";\nimport { timeline } from \"../../spec/timing\";\n\nconst MAX_WORDS = 5;\n// Breathing room so a caption never appears before its scene has settled or lingers past it.\nconst PAD_MS = 150;\n\nexport function buildCaptions(spec: VideoSpec): Caption[] {\n  const captions = spec.captions;\n  if (!captions?.enabled) return [];\n  if (captions.source === \"items\") return captions.items ?? [];\n  const fps = spec.format.fps;\n  return timeline(spec).entries.flatMap((entry) => {\n    if (!entry.scene.narration) return [];\n    const phrases = splitPhrases(entry.scene.narration);\n    const start = ((entry.from + entry.transitionIn) / fps) * 1000 + PAD_MS;\n    const end = ((entry.from + entry.durationInFrames) / fps) * 1000 - PAD_MS;\n    const weights = phrases.map((phrase) => wordCount(phrase) + 1);\n    const total = weights.reduce((sum, weight) => sum + weight, 0);\n    return phrases.map((phrase, index) => {\n      const before = weights\n        .slice(0, index)\n        .reduce((sum, weight) => sum + weight, 0);\n      const startMs = Math.round(start + ((end - start) * before) / total);\n      const endMs = Math.round(\n        start + ((end - start) * (before + (weights[index] ?? 0))) / total,\n      );\n      return {\n        text: phrase,\n        startMs,\n        endMs,\n        timestampMs: startMs,\n        confidence: null,\n      };\n    });\n  });\n}\n\n/** Short, readable phrases that break at natural pauses and never run past five words. */\nexport function splitPhrases(text: string) {\n  const words = text.trim().split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean);\n  const phrases = words.reduce<string[][]>(\n    (list, word) => {\n      const current = list[list.length - 1] ?? [];\n      const previous = current[current.length - 1];\n      // A sentence end always breaks; a comma only once the phrase has two words, so no caption\n      // is a lone word that flashes past too fast to read.\n      const sentence = previous !== undefined && /[.!?]$/.test(previous);\n      const pause =\n        previous !== undefined &&\n        /[;:,]$/.test(previous) &&\n        current.length >= 2;\n      const full = current.length >= MAX_WORDS || sentence || pause;\n      if (full && current.length > 0) return [...list, [word]];\n      return [...list.slice(0, -1), [...current, word]];\n    },\n    [[]],\n  );\n  // A single word stranded by the cap joins the phrase before it; one extra word reads better\n  // than a caption that flashes past too fast to see.\n  const merged = phrases\n    .filter((phrase) => phrase.length > 0)\n    .reduce<string[][]>((list, phrase) => {\n      const previous = list[list.length - 1];\n      const stranded =\n        phrase.length === 1 &&\n        previous !== undefined &&\n        previous.length === MAX_WORDS &&\n        !/[.!?]$/.test(previous[previous.length - 1] ?? \"\");\n      return stranded\n        ? Markers may span several words. */\nexport function parseEmphasis(text: string): Segment[] {\n  return text\n    .split(/(\\*[^*]+\\*)/)\n    .filter(Boolean)\n    .map((part) =>\n      part.startsWith(\"*\") && part.endsWith(\"*\") && part.length > 2\n        ? { text: part.slice(1, -1), emphasis: true }\n        : { text: part, emphasis: false },\n    );\n}\n\nfunction wordCount(text: string) {\n  return text.split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;\n}\n"},"src/lib/context.tsx":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"/**\n * Everything a component needs to look right in this video: the theme, the frame it is drawn\n * into, and the pacing. A transition belongs to the\n * scene it leads into and overlaps the end of the scene before it, matching TransitionSeries.\n */\nimport type { Scene, VideoSpec } from \"./schema\";\n\nexport type TimelineEntry = {\n  scene: Scene;\n  index: number;\n  /** First frame of the scene, including any transition into it. */\n  from: number;\n  durationInFrames: number;\n  /** Frames shared with the previous scene while the transition plays. */\n  transitionIn: number;\n};\n\nexport type Timeline = { entries: TimelineEntry[]; durationInFrames: number };\n\nexport function toFrames(seconds: number, fps: number) {\n  return Math.round(seconds * fps);\n}\n\nexport function transitionFrames(scene: Scene, index: number, fps: number) {\n  if (index === 0 || !scene.transition || scene.transition.type === \"none\")\n    return 0;\n  return toFrames(scene.transition.durationInSeconds, fps);\n}\n\nexport function timeline(spec: VideoSpec): Timeline {\n  const fps = spec.format.fps;\n  const entries = spec.scenes.reduce<TimelineEntry[]>((list, scene, index) => {\n    const previous = list[list.length - 1];\n    const transitionIn = transitionFrames(scene, index, fps);\n    const from = previous\n      ? previous.from + previous.durationInFrames - transitionIn\n      : 0;\n    return [\n      ...list,\n      {\n        scene,\n        index,\n        from,\n        durationInFrames: toFrames(scene.durationInSeconds, fps),\n        transitionIn,\n      },\n    ];\n  }, []);\n  const last = entries[entries.length - 1];\n  return {\n    entries,\n    durationInFrames: last ? last.from + last.durationInFrames : 0,\n  };\n}\n\n/** The scene on screen at `frame`. During a transition that is the incoming scene. */\nexport function sceneAt(spec: VideoSpec, frame: number) {\n  return timeline(spec).entries.findLast(\n    (entry) =>\n      frame >= entry.from && frame < entry.from + entry.durationInFrames,\n  );\n}\n"},"src/spec/validate.test.ts":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"import assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport { test } from \"node:test\";\nimport { defineVideo, type VideoSpecInput } from \"./schema\";\nimport { assetRefs, validate } from \"./validate\";\n\nconst make = (\n  patch: Partial<VideoSpecInput> = {},\n  scenes?: VideoSpecInput[\"scenes\"],\n) =>\n  defineVideo({\n    id: \"v\",\n    title: \"v\",\n    format: { width: 1080, height: 1920, fps: 30 },\n    style: {},\n    scenes: scenes ?? Errors block\n * rendering; warnings are worth a look but can be right on purpose. Pure: file checks live in\n * scripts/lib/assets.ts because the browser bundle cannot touch the disk.\n */\nimport {\n  codeCharsPerLine,\n  contentBox,\n  estimateLines,\n  monoCharsPerLine,\n  terminalFontSize,\n  typeSize,\n  type Frame,\n} from \"../styles/scale\";\nimport type { Scene, VideoSpec } from \"./schema\";\nimport { timeline } from \"./timing\";\n\nexport type Issue = { path: string; message: string };\nexport type Validation = { errors: Issue[]; warnings: Issue[] };\n\n// Average silent reading is about 240 words a minute. The lead-in covers finding the text.\nconst READ_WORDS_PER_SECOND = 4;\nconst NARRATION_WORDS_PER_SECOND = 3.3;\nconst READ_LEAD_IN_SECONDS = 0.8;\n// Two lines at caption size across a phone.\nconst MAX_CAPTION_CHARS = 42;\n// Past this a code listing is a wall, not a point.\nconst MAX_CODE_LINES = 14;\n\nexport const VIDEO_FILE = /\\.(mp4|webm|mov|mkv)$/i;\n\nexport function validate(spec: VideoSpec): Validation {\n  const errors: Issue[] = [];\n  const warnings: Issue[] = [];\n  const frame = { width: spec.format.width, height: spec.format.height };\n  const plan = timeline(spec);\n  const totalSeconds = plan.durationInFrames / spec.format.fps;\n\n  checkFormat(spec, errors);\n\n  spec.scenes.forEach((scene, index) => {\n    const path = `scenes[${index}]`;\n    if (spec.scenes.findIndex((other) => other.id === scene.id) !== index)\n      errors.push({\n        path: `${path}.id`,\n        message: `duplicate scene id \"${scene.id}\"`,\n      });\n\n    const previous = spec.scenes[index - 1];\n    const overlap = plan.entries[index]?.transitionIn ?? 0;\n    if (previous && overlap > 0) {\n      const seconds = overlap / spec.format.fps;\n      const shortest = Math.min(\n        previous.durationInSeconds,\n        scene.durationInSeconds,\n      );\n      if (seconds >= shortest)\n        errors.push({\n          path: `${path}.transition`,\n          message: `${seconds}s transition is longer than the ${shortest}s scene it joins`,\n        });\n    }\n\n    const words = visibleWords(scene);\n    const needed = READ_LEAD_IN_SECONDS + words / READ_WORDS_PER_SECOND;\n    if (words > 0 && scene.durationInSeconds < needed)\n      warnings.push({\n        path,\n        message: `on screen for ${scene.durationInSeconds}s, needs about ${needed.toFixed(1)}s to read ${words} words`,\n      });\n\n    if (\n      scene.narration &&\n      spec.captions?.enabled &&\n      spec.captions.source === \"narration\"\n    ) {\n      const rate = countWords(scene.narration) / scene.durationInSeconds;\n      if (rate > NARRATION_WORDS_PER_SECOND)\n        warnings.push({\n          path: `${path}.narration`,\n          message: `${rate.toFixed(1)} words/s is too fast to read as captions; aim for under ${NARRATION_WORDS_PER_SECOND}`,\n        });\n    }\n\n    checkFit(scene, path, frame, errors);\n    checkData(scene, path, errors);\n  });\n\n  if (\n    spec.targetDurationInSeconds !== undefined &&\n    Math.abs(totalSeconds - spec.targetDurationInSeconds) > 0.25\n  )\n    errors.push({\n      path: \"targetDurationInSeconds\",\n      message: `scenes add up to ${round(totalSeconds)}s, brief asks for ${spec.targetDurationInSeconds}s (${totalSeconds > spec.targetDurationInSeconds ? \"cut\" : \"add\"} ${round(Math.abs(totalSeconds - spec.targetDurationInSeconds))}s; transitions overlap scenes)`,\n    });\n\n  checkCaptions(spec, totalSeconds, errors, warnings);\n  return { errors, warnings };\n}\n\nexport type AssetRef = {\n  src: string;\n  path: string;\n  kind: \"image\" | \"video\" | \"audio\";\n};\n\n/** Every file in public/ the spec depends on. */\nexport function assetRefs(spec: VideoSpec): AssetRef[] {\n  const scenes = spec.scenes.flatMap((scene, index): AssetRef[] => {\n    if (scene.type === \"browser\" && scene.image)\n      return [\n        { src: scene.image, path: `scenes[${index}].image`, kind: \"image\" },\n      ];\n    if (scene.type === \"media\")\n      return [\n        {\n          src: scene.src,\n          path: `scenes[${index}].src`,\n          kind: VIDEO_FILE.test(scene.src) ? \"video\" : \"image\",\n        },\n      ];\n    if (scene.type === \"cta\" && scene.logo)\n      return [\n        { src: scene.logo, path: `scenes[${index}].logo`, kind: \"image\" },\n      ];\n    return [];\n  });\n  const audio = spec.audio;\n  const tracks: AssetRef[] = [\n    ...(audio?.music\n      ? [{ src: audio.music.src, path: \"audio.music\", kind: \"audio\" as const }]\n      : []),\n    ...(audio?.voiceover\n      ? [\n          {\n            src: audio.voiceover.src,\n            path: \"audio.voiceover\",\n            kind: \"audio\" as const,\n          },\n        ]\n      : []),\n    ...(audio?.effects ?? []).map((track, index) => ({\n      src: track.src,\n      path: `audio.effects[${index}]`,\n      kind: \"audio\" as const,\n    })),\n  ];\n  return [...scenes, ...tracks];\n}\n\nfunction checkFormat(spec: VideoSpec, errors: Issue[]) {\n  const { width, height, fps } = spec.format;\n  (\n    [\n      [\"width\", width],\n      [\"height\", height],\n    ] as const\n  ).forEach(([name, value]) => {\n    if (value % 2 !== 0)\n      errors.push({\n        path: `format.${name}`,\n        message: `${value} must be even for H.264 MP4 output`,\n      });\n    if (value < 240 || value > 4096)\n      errors.push({\n        path: `format.${name}`,\n        message: `${value} is outside 240–4096`,\n      });\n  });\n  if (fps < 12 || fps > 120)\n    errors.push({\n      path: \"format.fps\",\n      message: `${fps} fps is outside 12–120`,\n    });\n}\n\n/** The copy that has to fit, at the size its scene sets it. */\nfunction checkFit(scene: Scene, path: string, frame: Frame, errors: Issue[]) {\n  const box = contentBox(frame);\n  const vertical = frame.height > frame.width;\n  const fit = (\n    field: string,\n    text: string | undefined,\n    role: \"display\" | \"headline\" | \"title\",\n    maxLines: number,\n  ) => {\n    if (!text) return;\n    const lines = estimateLines(text, typeSize(frame, role), box.width);\n    if (lines > maxLines)\n      errors.push({\n        path: `${path}.${field}`,\n        message: `wraps to ${lines} lines at ${role} size, more than the ${maxLines} that fit; shorten it`,\n      });\n  };\n  if (scene.type === \"title\")\n    fit(\"headline\", scene.headline, \"display\", vertical ? 6 : 3);\n  if (scene.type === \"statement\")\n    scene.lines.forEach((line, index) =>\n      fit(`lines[${index}]`, line, \"headline\", vertical ? 3 : 2),\n    );\n  if (scene.type === \"cta\") fit(\"headline\", scene.headline, \"headline\", 3);\n  if (scene.type === \"quote\")\n    fit(\"quote\", scene.quote, \"title\", vertical ? 8 : 5);\n  if (\"heading\" in scene && scene.heading)\n    fit(\"heading\", scene.heading, \"title\", vertical ? 3 : 2);\n  // Mono text in a window cannot rewrap gracefully; a long line breaks mid-token.\n  if (scene.type === \"terminal\") {\n    const max = monoCharsPerLine(frame, terminalFontSize(frame)) - 2;\n    scene.lines.forEach((line, index) => {\n      if (line.text.length > max)\n        errors.push({\n          path: `${path}.lines[${index}]`,\n          message: `${line.text.length} characters, but ${max} fit on a terminal line; shorten it`,\n        });\n    });\n  }\n  if (scene.type === \"code\") {\n    const lines = scene.code.split(\"\\n\");\n    const max = codeCharsPerLine(frame, lines.length);\n    lines.forEach((line, index) => {\n      if (line.length > max)\n        errors.push({\n          path: `${path}.code`,\n          message: `line ${index + 1} has ${line.length} characters, but ${max} fit; wrap or shorten it`,\n        });\n    });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction checkData(scene: Scene, path: string, errors: Issue[]) {\n  if (scene.type === \"heatmap\")\n    scene.matrices.forEach((matrix, index) => {\n      const shaped =\n        matrix.values.length === matrix.rows.length &&\n        matrix.values.every((row) => row.length === matrix.cols.length);\n      if (!shaped)\n        errors.push({\n          path: `${path}.matrices[${index}].values`,\n          message: `expected ${matrix.rows.length} rows of ${matrix.cols.length} values`,\n        });\n    });\n  if (scene.type === \"flow\" && scene.loop) {\n    const missing = [scene.loop.from, scene.loop.to].find(\n      (step) => step < 0 || step >= scene.steps.length,\n    );\n    if (missing !== undefined)\n      errors.push({\n        path: `${path}.loop`,\n        message: `step ${missing} does not exist`,\n      });\n  }\n  if (\n    scene.type === \"chart\" &&\n    scene.highlight &&\n    !scene.data.some((item) => item.label === scene.highlight)\n  )\n    errors.push({\n      path: `${path}.highlight`,\n      message: `no bar labelled \"${scene.highlight}\"`,\n    });\n  if (\n    scene.type === \"vectors\" &&\n    new Set(scene.rows.map((row) => row.values.length)).size > 1\n  )\n    errors.push({\n      path: `${path}.rows`,\n      message: \"every vector needs the same number of values\",\n    });\n  if (scene.type === \"code\" && scene.code.split(\"\\n\").length > MAX_CODE_LINES)\n    errors.push({\n      path: `${path}.code`,\n      message: `more than ${MAX_CODE_LINES} lines will not be readable; show the part that matters`,\n    });\n}\n\nfunction checkCaptions(\n  spec: VideoSpec,\n  totalSeconds: number,\n  errors: Issue[],\n  warnings: Issue[],\n) {\n  const captions = spec.captions;\n  if (!captions?.enabled) return;\n  if (captions.source === \"items\" && !captions.items?.length)\n    errors.push({\n      path: \"captions.items\",\n      message: 'source is \"items\" but no captions were given',\n    });\n  (captions.source === \"items\" ? (captions.items ?? []) : []).forEach(\n    (item, index) => {\n      if (item.endMs / 1000 > totalSeconds + 0.01)\n        errors.push({\n          path: `captions.items[${index}]`,\n          message: `ends at ${round(item.endMs / 1000)}s, after the video ends at ${round(totalSeconds)}s`,\n        });\n      if (item.text.length > MAX_CAPTION_CHARS)\n        warnings.push({\n          path: `captions.items[${index}]`,\n          message: `over ${MAX_CAPTION_CHARS} characters wraps past two caption lines`,\n        });\n    },\n  );\n}\n\nfunction visibleWords(scene: Scene) {\n  const parts: (string | undefined)[] = (() => {\n    switch (scene.type) {\n      case \"title\":\n        return [scene.eyebrow, scene.headline, scene.subheadline];\n      case \"statement\":\n        return scene.lines;\n      case \"terminal\":\n        return [scene.heading, ...scene.lines.map((line) => line.text)];\n      case \"list\":\n        return [\n          scene.heading,\n          ...scene.items.flatMap((item) => [item.label, item.detail]),\n        ];\n      case \"cards\":\n        return [\n          scene.heading,\n          ...scene.cards.flatMap((card) => [card.title, card.body]),\n        ];\n      case \"flow\":\n        return [scene.heading, ...scene.steps, scene.loop?.label];\n      case \"metrics\":\n        return [scene.heading, ...scene.metrics.map((metric) => metric.label)];\n      case \"chart\":\n        return [scene.heading, ...scene.data.map((item) => item.label)];\n      case \"chips\":\n        return [\n          scene.heading,\n          ...scene.chips.map((chip) => chip.label),\n          scene.note,\n        ];\n      case \"vectors\":\n        return [\n          scene.heading,\n          ...scene.rows.map((row) => row.label),\n          scene.note,\n        ];\n      case \"heatmap\":\n        return [scene.heading, scene.note];\n      case \"code\":\n        return [scene.heading];\n      case \"browser\":\n        return [scene.heading, scene.page?.title, ...(scene.page?.lines ?? [])];\n      case \"media\":\n        return [scene.heading, scene.caption];\n      case \"quote\":\n        return [scene.quote, scene.author, scene.role];\n      case \"cta\":\n        return [scene.headline, scene.subline, scene.url];\n      default:\n        return unhandled(scene);\n    }\n  })();\n  return countWords(parts.filter(Boolean).join(\" \"));\n}\n\n/** Words a viewer reads; separators such as \"·\" and \"→\" are not words. */\nfunction countWords(text: string) {\n  return text.split(/\\s+/).filter((word) => /[\\p{L}\\p{N}]/u.test(word)).length;\n}\n\nfunction round(value: number) {\n  return Math.round(value * 100) / 100;\n}\n\n/** Compile-time guard: a new scene type fails here until validation knows its words. */\nfunction unhandled(scene: never): never {\n  throw new Error(`Unknown scene ${JSON.stringify(scene)}`);\n}\n"},"src/styles/scale.ts":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"/**\n * Sizes derived from the frame, never hard-coded per scene. Everything scales from the short\n * edge, so a 1080×1920 vertical and a 1920×1080 horizontal share one readable type scale.\n * Pure, so validation scripts can use it without loading React.\n */\n\nexport type Frame = { width: number; height: number };\n\nexport type TypeRole =\n  | \"display\"\n  | \"headline\"\n  | \"title\"\n  | \"body\"\n  | \"label\"\n  | \"caption\"\n  | \"mono\";\n\n// At a 1080px short edge. Copy a viewer must read to follow the video stays at or above the\n// skill's 44px floor (84px for headlines). `label` and `mono` are below it on purpose and are\n// for tertiary marks only: eyebrows, window titles, IDs, diagram axes.\nconst TYPE_AT_1080: Record<TypeRole, number> = {\n  display: 132,\n  headline: 96,\n  title: 64,\n  body: 46,\n  label: 32,\n  caption: 54,\n  mono: 36,\n};\n\nexport const LINE_HEIGHT: Record<TypeRole, number> = {\n  display: 1.02,\n  headline: 1.08,\n  title: 1.15,\n  body: 1.35,\n  label: 1.3,\n  caption: 1.2,\n  mono: 1.5,\n};\n\nexport function unit(frame: Frame) {\n  return Math.min(frame.width, frame.height) / 1080;\n}\n\nexport function typeSize(frame: Frame, role: TypeRole) {\n  return Math.round(TYPE_AT_1080[role] * unit(frame));\n}\n\n/** Spacing on an 8px grid at 1080. */\nexport function space(frame: Frame, steps: number) {\n  return Math.round(8 * steps * unit(frame));\n}\n\nexport type Insets = {\n  top: number;\n  right: number;\n  bottom: number;\n  left: number;\n};\n\n/**\n * Where important content may go. Vertical video reserves the bottom and right edges that\n * TikTok, Reels and Shorts cover with their own UI.\n */\nexport function safeArea(frame: Frame): Insets {\n  const u = unit(frame);\n  if (frame.height > frame.width) {\n    return {\n      top: Math.round(200 * u),\n      right: Math.round(120 * u),\n      bottom: Math.round(420 * u),\n      left: Math.round(100 * u),\n    };\n  }\n  return {\n    top: Math.round(100 * u),\n    right: Math.round(120 * u),\n    bottom: Math.round(110 * u),\n    left: Math.round(120 * u),\n  };\n}\n\nexport function contentBox(frame: Frame) {\n  const inset = safeArea(frame);\n  return {\n    width: frame.width - inset.left - inset.right,\n    height: frame.height - inset.top - inset.bottom,\n  };\n}\n\n// Average advance width as a share of font size. Inter's is about 0.52 for mixed-case text;\n// mono is fixed at 0.6. Deliberately a little generous so estimates err toward \"too long\".\nconst ADVANCE = { sans: 0.55, mono: 0.6 } as const;\n\n/**\n * Lines a text block will wrap to at a width. An estimate, used by validation to catch copy\n * that cannot fit before anything renders; the frames step is the real check.\n */\nexport function estimateLines(\n  text: string,\n  fontSize: number,\n  width: number,\n  family: keyof typeof ADVANCE = \"sans\",\n) {\n  const perLine = Math.max(1, Math.floor(width / (fontSize * ADVANCE[family])));\n  const words = text.replaceAll(\"*\", \"\").split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean);\n  return words.reduce(\n    (state, word) => {\n      const length =\n        state.current === 0 ? word.length : state.current + 1 + word.length;\n      if (length <= perLine || state.current === 0)\n        return { lines: state.lines, current: Math.min(length, perLine) };\n      return { lines: state.lines + 1, current: word.length };\n    },\n    { lines: words.length ? 1 : 0, current: 0 },\n  ).lines;\n}\n\n/** Terminal text: a little larger on vertical, where the window is the whole story. */\nexport function terminalFontSize(frame: Frame) {\n  return typeSize(frame, \"mono\") * (frame.height > frame.width ? 1.25 : 1);\n}\n\n/** Code text shrinks slightly for longer listings. */\nexport function codeFontSize(frame: Frame, lines: number) {\n  return typeSize(frame, \"mono\") * (lines > 10 ? 0.85 : 1);\n}\n\n/** Characters of mono text that fit on one line inside a window drawn in the content box. */\nexport function monoCharsPerLine(frame: Frame, fontSize: number, gutter = 0) {\n  const inner = contentBox(frame).width - 2 * space(frame, 4) - gutter;\n  return Math.floor(inner / (fontSize * ADVANCE.mono));\n}\n\n/** The line-number column and padding a CodeEditor draws to the left of each line. */\nexport function codeGutter(frame: Frame, fontSize: number) {\n  return (\n    fontSize * 1.4 +\n    space(frame, 3) +\n    space(frame, 3) +\n    space(frame, 4) +\n    space(frame, 0.5)\n  );\n}\n\n/** Characters that fit on one line of code in a CodeEditor, matching what it renders. */\nexport function codeCharsPerLine(frame: Frame, lines: number) {\n  const size = codeFontSize(frame, lines);\n  return Math.floor(\n    (contentBox(frame).width - codeGutter(frame, size)) / (size * ADVANCE.mono),\n  );\n}\n"},"src/styles/theme.test.ts":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"import assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport { test } from \"node:test\";\nimport { defineVideo } from \"../spec/schema\";\nimport { themeFor } from \"./theme\";\n\nconst spec = (style: Record<string, unknown>) =>\n  defineVideo({\n    id: \"t\",\n    title: \"t\",\n    format: { width: 1080, height: 1920 },\n    style,\n    scenes: [{ id: \"a\", type: \"title\", headline: \"A\", durationInSeconds: 2 }],\n  });\n\ntest(\"a brand palette overrides the theme's colours and nothing else\", () => {\n  const theme = themeFor(\n    spec({\n      theme: \"terminal\",\n      palette: { background: \"#101820\", accent: \"#FEE715\" },\n    }),\n  );\n  assert.equal(theme.palette.background, \"#101820\");\n  assert.equal(theme.palette.accent, \"#FEE715\");\n  assert.equal(theme.palette.accentSoft, \"rgba(254, 231, 21, 0.14)\");\n  assert.equal(\n    theme.palette.text,\n    themeFor(spec({ theme: \"terminal\" })).palette.text,\n  );\n});\n\ntest(\"the accent shorthand still works and palette wins over it\", () => {\n  assert.equal(themeFor(spec({ accent: \"#FF0000\" })).palette.accent, \"#FF0000\");\n  assert.equal(\n    themeFor(spec({ accent: \"#FF0000\", palette: { accent: \"#00FF00\" } }))\n      .palette.accent,\n    \"#00FF00\",\n  );\n});\n"},"src/styles/theme.ts":{"encoding":"utf8","data":"/**\n * The visual language.
